pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.queryid,

pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.total_exec_time / 1000.0 as seconds_total,
pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.rows as rows_total,
pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.blk_read_time / 1000.0 as block_read_seconds_total,

pg-1  | 			pg_get_userbyid(userid) as user,
pg-1  | 			pg_database.datname,
pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.queryid,
pg-1  | 			LEFT(pg_stat_statements.query, 120) as query,
pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.calls as calls_total,
pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.total_exec_time / 1000.0 as seconds_total,
pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.rows as rows_total,
pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.blk_read_time / 1000.0 as block_read_seconds_total,
pg-1  | 			pg_stat_statements.blk_write_time / 1000.0 as block_write_seconds_total
pg-1  | 			FROM pg_stat_statements(true)
pg-1  | 		JOIN pg_database
pg-1  | 			ON pg_database.oid = pg_stat_statements.dbid
pg-1  | 		WHERE
pg-1  | 			total_exec_time > (
pg-1  | 			SELECT percentile_cont(0.1)
pg-1  | 				WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY total_exec_time)
pg-1  | 				FROM pg_stat_statements(false)
pg-1  | 			)
pg-1  | 			 
pg-1  | 		ORDER BY seconds_total DESC
pg-1  | 		LIMIT 100;
